Title: Nova collectio statutorum ordinis Cartusiensis, ea quae in antiquis et novis statutis, ac tertia compilatione dispersa et confusa habebantur simul ordinate disposita complectens
Creator: The Carthusian Order (its general chapter)
Publisher: La Correrie (Correriae): Laurentius Gilibert, sworn printer at Grenoble, 1681 (title page: 'M. DC. LXXXI')
Publication Date: 1681 (title page, read directly)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1681); the archive.org record carries the Public Domain Mark (licenseurl publicdomain/mark/1.0)
Language: lat
Source: archive.org item bub_gb_UiYY_oQ31bEC, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-10 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (787,288) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1681, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair to garbled. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: The Carthusians' own statutes gathered into one order: the old and new statutes and the third compilation. The general chapter of 1679 is named at about OCR line 47.
